*Verse 10.* The Sun has traversed the first half of <term>Dhanus</term> (Sagittarius); the Moon is just there; Saturn possessed of much power is in the <term>Lagna</term> and Mars is in the exaltation sign <insert>Capricorn</insert>. If this be the planetary position at a person's birth, he will grow into such a mighty king that his enemies, overwhelmed by his fiery valour will do homage to him from afar, regarding him with terror.

->If the Moon, brilliant with a disc resembling the beauty of nectar and lotus stalks, occupies a Navamsa of the Sun, a ruler of the earth is born with many elephants, provided benefics are in the quadrants and not joined by malefics.<-

*Verse 11.* If the Moon brilliant with digits resembling mortar and lotus stalk in colour (*i. e.*, the full moon) occupy a <term>Navamsa</term><note>A one-ninth division of a zodiac sign, used for fine-tuning predictions.</note> owned by the Sun and when <term>benefics</term><note>Auspicous planets like Jupiter, Venus, or a strong Mercury.</note> occupy the <term>Kendras</term><note>The 1st, 4th, 7th, and 10th houses, considered the pillars of a horoscope.</note> and are not joined by <term>malefics</term><note>Inauspicious planets like Saturn or Mars.</note>, the person becomes a king possessing many elephants.

<header>Verses 15-18: Chapter Seven</header>

*Verse 15.* Venus, Jupiter, and Saturn are in <term>Meena</term> <note>the sign of Pisces</note>. The Moon, being almost full, is in its sign of exaltation. The Sun is aspected by Mars, and the sign <term>Mesha</term> <note>Aries</note> is rising as the ascendant. A person born under the above <term>yoga</term> <note>planetary combination</note> will become a king commanding a vast army. The march of his troops raises such a massive quantity of dust that it obscures the Sun; as a result, the lotus flowers begin to close, mistakenly believing the Sun has already set.

->If the <term>benefics</term> <note>auspicious planets like Jupiter, Venus, or Mercury</note> are in their signs of depression or in the houses of enemies,<-
->or if they occupy the eleventh, sixth, or third houses; or if they are in their highest exaltation,<-
->bright and clear in the <term>Kendras</term> <note>the 1st, 4th, 7th, or 10th houses</note>; and if the Moon is in<-
->the sign of <term>Kataka</term> <note>Cancer</note> as the tenth house during a night birth,<-
->that person will be a lord of warriors, ruling the three worlds under a single royal umbrella. 16.<-

*Verse 16.* If there be a birth at night when <term>benefics</term> uneclipsed are in depression or inimical houses identical with the 11th, or occupy the 6th and 3rd houses, or be in their highest exaltation, or be all posited in the <term>Kendras</term>, and if the Moon be in <term>Kataka</term> identical with the 10th house, the person born will become an emperor ruling the three worlds under one umbrella <note>a symbol of universal sovereignty</note>.

->If the full Moon occupies a <term>Vargottama amsa</term>, it makes one a king of unprecedented and pleasing fame,<-
->the dust raised by the hooves of whose many horses overpowers the Sun,<-
->making it resemble the pale form of the Moon at dawn.<-

*Verse 17.* If the Moon with full digits (full Moon) occupy a <term>Vargottama amsa</term> <note>a specific division where the planet remains in the same sign in both the main chart and the 9th divisional chart</note>, the person born will become a mighty ruler of the earth and his fame will be immense. He will command a good number of horses, the dusts raised by whose hoofs will so overpower the Sun that he will resemble the Moon at early dawn.

->If Jupiter and the Moon are in the <term>Kendras</term> at birth and are aspected by Venus,<-
->one becomes a king of incomparable fame, provided no planet is in its sign of depression. 18.<-

*Verse 18.* If at a birth, Jupiter and the Moon occupy <insert>the <term>Kendras</term> and are aspected by Venus, the person becomes a king of matchless fame, provided no planet in the chart is in its sign of depression.</insert>

# Phaladeepika Chapter VII

<page-num>78</page-num>
<header>Phaladeepika Chapter VII</header>

...occupying a <term>Kendra</term><note>A "Kendra" refers to the 1st, 4th, 7th, or 10th houses of a horoscope, considered the pillars of a chart.</note> be aspected by Venus and there be no planet in depression <note>original: "neecha" or debilitation</note>, the native will become a king whose fame will be unparalleled.

->If the Moon occupies a water-residing sign or <term>Navamsa</term><note>A one-ninth divisional chart used for fine-tuning the strength of planets.</note> in the first house, and is in a beneficial or its own division,<-
->it makes one a king who does good; however, if it is not in a <term>Kendra</term>, he becomes a king with many elephants who is cruel.<-

*Sloka 19* If the Moon occupy a water-resorting <term>Rasi</term><note>Zodiac sign.</note> or <term>Amsa</term> identical with the <term>Lagna</term><note>The Ascendant or the sign rising on the eastern horizon at birth.</note> or be in his own <term>varga</term><note>A divisional strength or category.</note>, the person born will become a king who will do good to his subjects. But should the Moon in the above <term>yoga</term><note>A specific planetary combination or "union."</note> occupy a house other than a <term>Kendra</term>, the native will turn out a king owning many elephants but oppressing his people.

->Venus aspected by Jupiter makes a scion of royalty a king;<-
->Jupiter in the first house (except in Capricorn) makes a king with rutting elephants.<-
->The Lord of the Ascendant in a <term>Kendra</term> with full strength makes a ruler of the earth;<-
->If Mercury is there aspected by Jupiter, other kings obey his commands.<-

<margin>||</margin> *Sloka 20* A scion of a royal family will become a king if at his birth Venus is aspected by Jupiter. Jupiter occupying a <term>Rasi</term> other than <term>Makara</term><note>The sign of Capricorn, where Jupiter is traditionally considered weak or "debilitated."</note> identical with the <term>Lagna</term> will usher a king endowed with elephants in rut. The lord of the <term>Lagna</term> occupying a <term>Kendra</term> in full strength will cause a ruler of men to be born. Mercury in the above position aspected by Jupiter will usher into the world a person whose orders will be obeyed by kings.

->Even a single planet in its exaltation sign aspected by a friend makes a king wealthy through friendly ties.<-
->If the Sun and Moon are both in their own signs, one becomes a ruler of a country with great wealth and elephants.<-

<margin>*</margin> *Sloka 21* A single planet occupying his highest exaltation point and aspected by friendly planets produces a king. Such a planet will make him immensely wealthy if he be also associated with another friendly <insert>planet</insert>. <note>The text continues to describe the power of the Sun and Moon in their respective home signs of Leo and Cancer.</note>

### Chapter VIII.

*Verse 1* If the Sun occupy the 1st house <note>The Ascendant or Lagna</note> at a person's birth, he will have very little hair; he will be most inactive to do any work, he will be angry and impetuous, and tall in stature. He will be proud, will have soiled eyes and a lean body. He will be valiant, impatient and cruel hearted. If the <term>Lagna</term> <note>The sign rising on the eastern horizon at birth</note> at birth be <term>Cancer</term> and the Sun occupy it, he will have a cataract in the eyes; if it be <term>Aries</term> <note>original: "Mesha"</note>, he will suffer from eye disease. If the Sun be in <term>Leo</term> identical with the <term>Lagna</term>, the person concerned will be night blind. If *Libra* be the <term>Lagna</term> and the Sun occupy it, he will suffer from poverty and loss of children.

---

->The Sun in the second house makes one devoid of learning, modesty, and wealth, and causes stammering speech.<-
->In the third house, the Sun makes one powerful, courageous, and wealthy, but an enemy to his own kin.<-
->In the fourth house, the Sun deprives one of happiness, relations, lands, and friends;<-
->it leads to service of the king and the squandering of ancestral wealth. || 2 ||<-

*Verse 2* When the Sun is in the 2nd house in a person's birth, he will be devoid of learning, modesty and riches and will be stammering. If the Sun should occupy the 3rd house, the person concerned will be strong, brave, wealthy and liberal, but inimical towards his relations. If the Sun be in the 4th house, the person born will have no happiness, no relations, no lands, no friends and no house. He will serve the State and spend away his paternal property.

---

->In the fifth house, the Sun grants happiness, wealth, and longevity, but denies children; it makes one wise but a wanderer of forests.<-
->In the sixth house, the Sun makes one a famous king, endowed with good qualities, wealth, and victory.<-
->In the seventh house, the Sun causes one to be hindered by the king, a wanderer, without a wife, and of frustrated intellect.<-
->In the eighth house, the Sun destroys wealth and longevity, and deprives one of friends and eyesight. || 3 ||<-

# Chapter 8, Verses 3-5

*Verse 3.* If the Sun should occupy the 5th house <note>The house of children, intelligence, and past merit</note> from the *Lagna* <note>The Ascendant or the sign rising on the eastern horizon at birth</note>, the person born will be bereft of happiness, riches, and children, and his life will be a short one. He will be intelligent and will travel over forest regions. If the Sun be in the 6th house <note>The house of enemies, debt, and disease</note>, the person born will become a king, famous, of <unclear>good</unclear> qualities, wealthy and victorious. If the Sun occupy the 7th house <note>The house of marriage and partnerships</note> at a birth, the person concerned will incur the displeasure of the sovereign, and will be deformed; he will be wandering, without a wife, and suffer humiliation. If the Sun should be in the 8th house <note>The house of longevity and hidden things</note>, the person born will lose his wealth and friends; he will not be long-lived and will have defective eyesight.

->If the Sun is in the 9th, one has children and kin but hates his father; he is devoted to Gods and Brahmins.<-
->In the 10th, he has sons, vehicles, praise, wit, wealth, power, and fame; he is a lord of the earth.<-
->When the Sun reaches the 11th, he has great wealth and long life, is free from grief, and is a leader of men.<-
->In the 12th house, he is an enemy to his father, has impaired vision, and lacks both wealth and sons. 4.<-
<note>original Sanskrit: "nijanako'rke sasutabandhustapasi devadvijamanāḥ..."</note>

*Verse 4.* If at the time of birth, the Sun should occupy the 9th house, the person concerned will hate his father; he will have children and relations and will revere Gods and Brahmins <note>The priestly or scholarly class</note>. If he occupies the 10th house, the person will have sons, vehicles, laudation, intelligence, wealth, strength, and fame. He will be a king. The Sun in the 11th house will make the person born very wealthy and long-lived. He will be a king and will have no sorrow. If the Sun be in the 12th house, the person born will hate his father. His eyesight will be defective and he will be without wealth and children.

->When the Moon is waxing in the Ascendant, one has a firm body, long life, and is fearless,<-
->strong, and wealthy; the opposite occurs if the Moon is waning.<-
<note>original Sanskrit: "site candre lagne dṛḍhatanuradaśrāyurabhayo..."</note>

# Phaladeepika, Chapter VIII

## One who is wealthy, respected by kin, fearless, and happy, yet defective in speech.
## In the third house, one has brothers, joy, strength, and valor, but is very miserly. || 5 ||

<margin>Moon</margin>
*Verse 5.* If at a person's birth, the Moon be waxing <note>The Sanskrit term implies the 'bright half' of the lunar cycle, considered auspicious</note> and happens to be in the first house, the person concerned will possess a strong constitution and a long lease of life. He will be free from fear, very powerful and wealthy. But if the Moon be waning <note>The 'dark half' of the lunar cycle</note>, the effects stated above will be quite the reverse. If the Moon should occupy the 2nd house, the person born will be rich and very learned; he will be a sensualist but defective of some limb. If the Moon be in the 3rd house, the person born will have brothers, will be lascivious, strong and powerful but miserly.

## Happy, enjoying pleasures, generous, with friends, vehicles, and fame.
## With good sons, wise, gentle in gait, and a minister in the fifth.
## In the sixth, the Moon brings short life, dullness, stomach disease, and defeat.
## In the seventh, under a gentle gaze, one has a beautiful wife and is very fortunate. || 6 ||

<margin>[</margin>
*Verse 6* If at a birth the Moon be in the 4th house, the person concerned will be happy and indulging in sensual pleasures. He will be liberal in gifts, will have friends, vehicles and become renowned. If the Moon should occupy the 5th house, the person born will have good sons, will be very intelligent, will walk gently and will become a minister <note>The term 'Amatya' suggests a high-ranking official or counselor to a king</note>. If the Moon be in the 6th house, the person born will be short-lived, ignorant, will suffer from dysentery, belly-ache, etc., he will also suffer humiliation. If the Moon should occupy the 7th house, the person born will be agreeable to look at, will be loved by a beautiful damsel, and will be exceedingly lovely.

->In the eighth house, one is sickly and short-lived; in the ninth, one is auspicious, virtuous, and possesses children.<-

**Verse 7** The person at whose birth the Moon is in the 8th house <term>8th house: traditionally associated with longevity and chronic illness</term> will suffer from diseases and will be short-lived. If the Moon should occupy the 9th house at a birth, the person concerned will be prosperous, virtuous and blessed with children. He will be victorious, and when he enters on a business, he will meet with success at the beginning itself. If the Moon should occupy the 10th house at a birth, the person concerned will do good acts and will be liked by the virtuous. If the Moon be in the 11th house, the person born will be high-minded, long-lived, and endowed with riches, children and servants. If the Moon be in the 12th house at a birth, the person concerned will be odious to others, he will suffer misery, will be insulted and will be most inactive.

->With a scarred body, very cruel, and short-lived in the first house; in the second, daring for wealth,<-
->Averse in speech, devoid of knowledge and riches, and dependent on wicked people.<-
->Possessing good qualities and wealth, brave, invincible, happy, and without younger siblings in the third;<-
->In the fourth, without friends, mother, land, happiness, or a home. || 8 ||<-

<margin>Mars</margin>
**Verse 8** If Mars occupy the <term>Lagna: the Ascendant or 1st house</term> at a person's birth, he will have an injured limb, he will be short-lived and will be very daring and rash. If Mars be in the 2nd house, the person concerned will be adverse (disinclined), devoid of learning and wealth and will be dependent on bad people. If Mars occupy the 3rd house, the person born will be of good qualities, possess wealth, will be brave, unassailable, happy and will have no younger brothers—

# Phaladeepika Chapter VIII

If at a birth Mars should occupy the 4th house <note>The 4th house represents the home, mother, and emotional foundations.</note>, the person concerned will be without friends, mother, lands, happiness, house, and vehicles.

->He is devoid of happiness and children, mostly beset by misfortunes; in the 5th house, he is a backbiter and of weak intellect.<-
->In the 6th, he is intensely passionate, wealthy, famous, a conqueror of enemies, and a leader.<-
->In the 7th, he performs improper acts, is afflicted by disease, wanders about, and loses his wife.<-
->In the 8th, he has a deformed body, is poor, short-lived, and condemned by the public. || 9 ||<-

*Verse 9* <note>original: "Sloha 9"</note> If at a birth Mars be in the 5th house, the person concerned will be unhappy, without children, full of reverses, backbiting and weak-minded. If Mars occupy the 6th house <note>The 6th house is traditionally associated with enemies, debt, and disease; Mars here is considered strong for overcoming obstacles.</note>, the person born will be exceedingly smitten with love, wealthy, and famous; he will be a king and victorious in battle. Mars in the 7th house makes the person born do improper acts, suffer affliction through disease, wander in the roads and lose his wife. If Mars be in the 8th house, the person born will have a deformed body, will be poor, short-lived and cursed by the people.

->Though a friend to the king, he is hated; he is fatherless in the 9th house and a harmer of people.<-
->In the 10th, he is a king, cruel, a giver, and praised by eminent men.<-
->In the 11th, he is endowed with wealth and happiness, free from grief, brave, and of good character.<-
->In the 12th, he has eye defects, is cruel, wifeless, a backbiter, and mean. || 10 ||<-

*Verse 10* <note>original: "Sloha 10"</note> If Mars occupy the 9th house <note>The 9th house governs fortune, religion, and the father.</note> at a person's birth, the native will, though a friend of the sovereign, be hated by others. He will be fatherless and will torment other people. Mars in the 10th house makes the person born a king, cruel, liberal <note>meaning generous or charitable</note> and praised by important people. If at a birth Mars should be in the 11th house, the person concerned will be endowed with riches and happiness. He will be brave, will have no <note>The text cuts off here, likely continuing with "sorrow" or "grief" on the following page.</note>

# Phaladeepika, Chapter VIII

*Verse 16* If Jupiter occupy the 9th house <note>The house of fortune and spirituality</note> at a person's birth, he will become a famous minister, will be endowed with wealth and children and will be anxious to do virtuous acts. If Jupiter should be in the 10th house <note>The house of profession and public standing</note>, the person born will follow the right course of conduct, will be renowned for his virtues, will become very rich and a friend of the king. When Jupiter is posited in the 11th house <note>The house of gains</note>, the person born will be wealthy, fearless, will have a few children, will be long-lived and will possess vehicles. If Jupiter be in the 12th house <note>The house of loss and expenditure</note>, the person concerned will be hated by others, will be foul-mouthed, will have no children, will be sinful, idle and a menial.

->Venus in the first house makes one handsome, beloved, and happy with a long life;<-
->In the second, it makes one a poet possessed of much wealth.<-
->In the third, one is without a wife, unhappy, miserly, and unpopular;<-
->In the fourth, one possesses good vehicles, houses, jewelry, clothes, and perfumes. || 17 ||<-

*Verse 17* If Venus should occupy the Ascendant <term>Lagna: the degree of the zodiac rising on the eastern horizon at the moment of birth</term> at a person's birth, he will have a healthy and beautiful body, he will be happy and endowed with long life. When Venus occupies the 2nd house, the person born will become a poet and endowed with riches. Venus in the 3rd house at birth makes the native wifeless, unhappy, poor, miserly and unpopular. If Venus should be in the 4th house at a birth, the native will have good vehicles, good house, jewels, clothes, scents etc.

->In the fifth house, one has unbroken wealth, is a king, wise, and has children;<-
->In the sixth, one is without enemies but poor, corrupted by young women, and distressed;<-
->In the seventh, one has a good wife but is devoted to others' wives, or loses a spouse yet remains wealthy;<-
->In the eighth, one is long-lived, a ruler of the earth, and wealthy. || 18 ||<-

*Verse 18* If Venus be in the 5th house at a birth, <note>The text continues to describe the effects of Venus in the 5th through 8th houses, noting themes of wealth, royalty, and domestic life.</note>

<note>original: "भृगुर्" (Bhrigu) is a name for the planet Venus used in the Sanskrit verse.</note>

**Verse 19.** If Venus occupy the 9th house at a birth, the person concerned will be blessed with a wife, friends and children, and will become prosperous through royal favor. If Venus should be in the 10th house, the person born will become widely renowned, will have friends, and will be a lord happily employed. <margin>}</margin>Venus in the 11th house makes the person born rich, fond of the company of other females and endowed with many comforts. When Venus occupies the 12th house, the person born will have sexual enjoyment, wealth and splendour.

->In the Ascendant, the person becomes equal to a king or the lord of a country or city;<-
->In the remaining houses, the child is surely afflicted by suffering in childhood,<-
->Subject to the pain of poverty, and is filthy and lazy. || 20 ||<-

*Sloka 23.* The person at whose birth Saturn is in the 8th house <note>The house of longevity and secrets, often associated with chronic ailments.</note> will be unclean and without wealth. He will suffer from piles <term>piles: hemorrhoids, a common translation for the Sanskrit 'Arshas'</term>, will be cruel-minded, will be pinched with hunger and will be despised by his friends.

->In the 9th, one lacks fortune, wealth, children, father, and righteousness; in the 10th, one is a minister or king, wealthy, devoted to agriculture, brave, and famous.<-

The person who has Saturn in the 11th house <note>The house of gains and ambitions.</note> in his nativity will have a long span of life, lasting wealth and good income, will be brave, free from disease and moneyed. Saturn in the 12th house <note>The house of loss and liberation.</note> makes the native impudent, indigent, without children, defective of some limb, stupid and driven out by his enemies.

*Sloka 25.* <note>This verse summarizes the effects of Saturn in the first four houses: the Ascendant (1st), the house of wealth (2nd), the house of courage/siblings (3rd), and the house of the home (4th).</note>

# Phaladeepika Chapter VIII

*Verse 25* If at a birth Rahu <note>The North Node of the Moon, considered a "shadow planet" in Vedic astrology</note> occupy the Ascendant <note>original: "Lagna"; the sign rising on the eastern horizon at birth</note>, the person concerned will have a short life, possess wealth and strength, and will suffer from diseases in the higher limbs of his body (head, face etc). The person who has at his birth Rahu in the 2nd house will be dubious and equivocal in his speech, will suffer from disease in the mouth or face, will be tender hearted, will get wealth through the sovereign <note>Likely referring to government or royal patronage</note>, will be wrathful and happy. Rahu in the 3rd house makes the person born proud, hostile to his brothers, strong willed, long lived and wealthy. If Rahu should occupy the 4th house, the person born will be a fool, will cause sorrow, will have friends, will be short lived and happy at some time or other.

->He speaks through his nose, is childless, and hard-hearted when Rahu is in the 5th; he suffers from stomach ailments.<-
->Oppressed by enemies and planetary spirits, suffering from rectal disease, yet wealthy and long-lived in the 6th.<-
->Losing wealth through women, distressed, impotent, independent but dull-witted in the 7th.<-
->In the 8th, he is short-lived, performs impure acts, is mutilated, suffers from wind-disorders, and has few children.<-
<note>This is a translation of the Sanskrit verse 26 provided in the source text.</note>

*Verse 26* If at a person's birth Rahu should be posited in the 5th house he will talk through the nose, will be childless, will be hard hearted and suffer from belly ache. If Rahu be in the 6th house the person born will be troubled by his enemies, or oppressed by malefic planets <note>original: "vidwat-pagraha-piditah"; often interpreted as being afflicted by spirits or specific planetary alignments</note>. He will suffer from a disease in the anus. He will be wealthy and long lived.
<margin>7th and 8th houses</margin> When Rahu occupies the 7th house, the person concerned will lose his wealth through intrigues with women, suffer separation from his beloved, lose his manhood <note>Likely referring to impotence or a loss of virility</note>, become self-willed and silly. If Rahu occupy the 8th house, the person born will be short lived, will do impure acts, will be defective of a limb, will suffer from wind disease <note>term: "Vata-amaya" - a traditional Ayurvedic classification for disorders related to the "wind" humor, such as arthritis, bloating, or nervous system issues</note> and will have limited issues <note>Meaning few children</note>.

<summary>This page details the astrological influences of Rahu when placed in the first through eighth houses, describing physical ailments, character traits, and life outcomes according to the Phaladeepika.</summary>

# Chapter Eight
## Verses 27-29

->**Verse 27**<-

If Rahu <note>The North Node of the Moon, often associated with worldly desire and sudden changes</note> happens to be in the **9th house** at a birth, the person concerned will speak in opposition to others. He will be the head of his clan, the headman of a village, or the mayor of a city, yet will commit unrighteous deeds. 

Rahu in the **10th house** makes the native famous; the man will have a limited number of children, will engage himself in the business of others, will not perform any good acts, and will be fearless. 

If Rahu is in the **11th house**, the person born will be prosperous, will have many children, will be long-lived, and will suffer from ear disease. 

If Rahu should occupy the **12th house**, the person concerned will be intent on committing sinful acts secretly, will spend much, and will suffer from a "water disease" <note>likely referring to edema or dropsy</note>.

->**Verse 28**<-

The person at whose birth Ketu <note>The South Node of the Moon, associated with spirituality or detachment, but often considered malefic in physical matters</note> occupies the **Lagna** <note>The Ascendant or 1st house</note> will be ungrateful, unhappy, and a carrier of tales to others. He will be an outcast, fallen from his position, will have a deformed body, and will associate with the wicked. 

Ketu in the **2nd house** makes the native devoid of learning and riches. His speech will be very vile in quality, and he will have a sinister look. He will forever be eating at the tables of others.

->**Verse 29**<-

Ketu in the **3rd house** confers on the native long life, strength, wealth, and fame. He will enjoy the company of a wife and good food, but this position causes the loss of his brothers. 

In the **4th house**, Ketu causes the loss of lands, fields, vehicles, mother, and happiness. It also results in the loss of one's birthplace and forces the native to live in the house of another.

*Verse 34.* A planet produces the full effect of the <term>bhava</term> <note>astrological house</note> in which it is when its distance (in <term>navamsas</term> <note>the ninth part of a sign</note>, degrees etc.) from the commencement of the <term>Rasi</term> <note>zodiac sign</note> occupied is equal to that traversed by the <term>Lagna</term> <note>the Ascendant or rising point</note> point in the Lagna Rasi. It is declared by the authorities that Rahu is similar to Saturn and Ketu to Mars in giving effects.

->Those situated in the same degree as the house-center provide the full house-result. According to whether the degrees are less or more, an increase or decrease in the result should be stated.<-

--- Page 120 ---
<meta>This page continues the transition from the discussion of planetary effects in the twelve houses to the specific characteristics of the twelve Zodiac signs (Rashis) when they serve as the Ascendant.</meta>

<page-num>98</page-num>
->Chapter IX of the Phaladeepika<-

The <term>Houses</term><note>original: "bhavas"; the twelve divisions of the sky representing different areas of life</note> are respectively occupied by the planets when they are exactly positioned in the <term>House-Center</term><note>original: "Bhavamsa"; the most powerful point or midpoint of an astrological house</note>. If the planets should occupy a position less or greater than this center point, the effects are declared to be correspondingly increasing or decreasing as the case may be.

Thus ends the 8th Chapter on "The effects of the Sun and other planets in the 12 houses from the Ascendant onwards" in the work *Phaladeepika* composed by Mantreswara.

## Chapter IX.

*Verse 1* The person at whose birth the sign <term>Aries</term><note>original: "Mesha"</note> is the <term>Ascendant</term><note>original: "Lagna"; the sign rising on the eastern horizon at the moment of birth</note> will have round eyes, will be weak-kneed, passionate, afraid of water, will eat sparingly, will long for women, will always be on his legs <insert>constantly wandering</insert>, will be fickle, will speak falsehoods, and will have bruised or scarred limbs.

<note>The Sanskrit verse describes the traits of those born under the sign of Taurus.</note>

*Verse 2.* If at the birth of a person, the sign <term>Taurus</term><note>original: "Vrishabha"</note> be rising, he will have broad thighs and a big face; he will be engaged in agriculture. He will be happy in the middle and concluding portions of his life. He will be fond of young women, will be liberal in gifts, will be of a forgiving disposition, will endure hardships, possess cattle and cows, and will have marks or moles on the back, face, and sides.

*Verse 9* If the <term>Lagna</term> be <term>Dhanus</term> <note>Sagittarius</note> at a person's birth, he will have a very long face and neck, his ears and nose will be big, he will be intent on his business, he will be of short stature, he will be the favourite of the king, he will be eloquent, he will be liberal in his gifts and will destroy his enemies. He can be won only by kind treatment and will be endowed with great strength.

->अधः कृशः सत्वयुतो गृहीतवाक्योऽलसोऽगम्यजराङ्गनेष्टः ।
धर्मध्वजो भाग्ययुतोऽटनश्च वातार्दितो नक्रभवो विलज्जः ॥ १० ॥<-

# Chapter Nine

<page-num>101</page-num>
->Verses 10-13<-

---

*Verse 10.* The following will be the characteristics of a person who is born when <term>Makara</term><note>The Sanskrit name for the zodiac sign Capricorn, often depicted as a sea-monster or crocodile-goat.</note> is rising. He will be weak in the lower limbs; he will have exceeding strength; he will understand and follow when once advised; he will be indolent; he will be attached to old women of the prohibited class. He will be interested in all religious matters. He will be fortunate; he will be always on his legs; he will suffer from <term>wind-disease</term><note>A reference to "Vata" imbalances in Ayurvedic medicine, often manifesting as joint pain, bloating, or nervous disorders.</note> and he will be devoid of all shame.

The one born under the sign of the Water-pot <insert>Aquarius</insert> commits sins in secret; his body is shaped like a jar; he is clever at causing obstructions and can endure long journeys. He possesses little wealth, is greedy, and spends the money of others. He experiences constant fluctuations of loss and gain, and is fond of fragrant perfumes and flowers.
<margin>Verse 11</margin>

*Verse 11.* The person at whose birth sign Aquarius is rising will secretly commit sinful deeds, will have a body similar to a water pot; will be intent on doing hindrance to others; will endure long marches on roads. His means will be very limited. He will be covetous and freely utilise other's wealth and will have pecuniary losses and gains, and will be fond of perfumes and flowers.

One who drinks excessive water, possesses a symmetrical and beautiful body, is devoted to his own wife, and enjoys wealth derived from the water. He is learned, grateful, overcomes his enemies, has beautiful eyes, and is fortunate when the final sign <insert>Pisces</insert> is rising.
<margin>Verse 12</margin>

*Verse 12.* If at a birth sign <term>Meena</term><note>The Sanskrit name for Pisces, represented by two fish.</note> be the <term>Lagna</term><note>The Ascendant or the sign rising on the eastern horizon at the moment of birth.</note>, the person born will drink water in excess quantity, will have a symmetrical and shining body, will be fond of his wife, will gain money by selling pearls and other produce of the ocean. He will be learned, feel grateful for past favours done to him, will overcome his enemies, possess very good eyes and will be fortunate.

Having understood the nature, habitat, form, and color of the signs, one should logically deduce the corresponding results. Whatever effects are declared for the rising sign should also be applied to the sign occupied by the Moon.
<margin>Verse 13</margin>

*Verse 13.* After examining the nature, place, form, and color of the signs, one should logically state the results. Whatever results are attributed here to the <term>Lagna</term> <note>The Ascendant</note> should also be declared for the <term>Chandra-lagna</term> <note>The sign where the Moon is positioned at birth</note>.

# Phaladeepika Chapter IX

Regarding the color and other characteristics of any particular <term>Rasi</term> <note>Zodiac sign</note>, one ought to intelligently guess the effects thereof. Whatever have been declared as the effects arising out of a particular sign being the <term>Lagna</term> <note>The Ascendant or the sign rising on the eastern horizon at birth</note>, the same should also be said to come to pass if the sign happens to be occupied by the Moon.

->If a planet is in its highest state of exaltation, the person becomes a master of the earth's treasures,<-
->praised by kings and a dwelling place of great wealth.<-
->Endowed with noble qualities, he triumphs like King Vikramarka<-
->in policy, fame, valor, generosity, and skillful courage. || 14 ||<-

*Verse 14* If at a birth a planet be in its exaltation <note>The position where a planet is at its maximum strength</note>, the person born will own precious stones and other jewels, will receive praises from a King, will be the store of valuable treasure, will be noble minded, and will shine like king Vikramarka <note>A legendary emperor of ancient India known for his wisdom and bravery</note> in all his courses of conduct, fame, valour, liberality, courage and cleverness.

->When a planet is in its own house, one gains wealth through the favor of a lord,<-
->or perhaps lordship itself, and a stable residence.<-
->In its specific time, one acquires a new house and fertile lands;<-
->one receives great respect from people and may even recover lost items. || 15 ||<-

*Verse 15* If a planet should occupy his own sign <margin>In a birth chart</margin> the person concerned will during the <term>dasa</term> <note>A major planetary period or life chapter in Vedic astrology</note> period of the said planet attain power and strength through the help of a wealthy personage or may himself become a lord, will stick up to his residence without moving anywhere, will acquire a new house as well as lands capable of yielding every kind of crop and will be honoured by his people. He may get back even lost articles.

->A planet in a friend's sign brings success in undertakings through friends and new friendships.<-
->It grants good children, a spouse, wealth, grain, and fortune, and ensures the favor of all people. || 16 ||<-

<summary>This page from the Phaladeepika outlines the auspicious results of planets being placed in their exaltation, their own signs, or friendly signs, promising wealth, power, and social success. It emphasizes that the Moon's position in a sign carries similar weight to the Ascendant.</summary>
<keywords>Phaladeepika, astrology, Rasi, Lagna, Vikramarka, dasa, planetary exaltation, wealth, Vedic astrology</keywords>

# Verse 16-19 / Chapter Nine

*Verse 16* The effect of a planet occupying a friends’ house in a <term>nativity</term> <note>A birth chart or horoscope.</note> will be to make the owner thereof gain success through his friends in all his attempts, cultivate new friendships, possess good sons, wife, wealth, corn and other fortunes and receive help from all people.

->When a planet enters the house of an enemy, it brings baseness, living on the food of others, and staying in the houses of others. It causes destitution and constant harassment by enemies; even a friend will become a great foe.<- <note>original: "गते ग्रहे शत्रुगृहं निकृष्टतां परान्नवृत्तिं परमन्दिरस्थितिम् । अकिंचनत्वं रिपुपीडनं सदा स्निग्धोऽपि तस्यातिरिपुत्वमाप्नुयात् ॥"</note>

*Verse 17.* If a planet should occupy an inimical sign, the person concerned will have a base disposition of mind. He will live in other people’s houses eating their food. He will be utterly destitute and will be always teased by enemies. Even a person who was originally his friend will prove inimical to him subsequently.

->When a planet is in its sign of depression, they say it causes a fall from status, humiliation by one's own servants, poverty, wicked conduct, and the accumulation of debts. It leads to seeking shelter with the lowly, living in a wretched land, servitude, wandering, and useless, harmful actions.<- <note>original: "नीचे ग्रहेऽधः पतनं स्वभृत्यैर्दैन्यं दुराचारमृणाप्तिमाहुः । नीचाश्रयं कीकटदेशवासं भृत्यत्वमध्वानमनर्थकार्यम् ॥ १८ ॥"</note>

*Verse 18* If a planet be in <term>depression</term> <note>The sign where a planet is at its weakest (Neecha).</note>, the native concerned will, during the planet’s <term>dasa period</term> <note>A major planetary cycle or time-period in a person's life.</note>, have a degradation from his position, will suffer humiliation, will do sinful deeds, will contract debts, will seek help from low people, dwell in <term>insanitary surroundings</term> <note>original: "kikatadesha," historically referring to a region considered impure or unholy.</note>, will do menial work, will walk long distances and commit rash acts.

->A planet that has reached a state of eclipse causes death shortly, or the loss of wife, children, and wealth; it brings useless quarrels, blame, and disgrace. A planet in an even sign does not produce special results, but produces happiness or sorrow in a steady, unchanging manner as before.<- <note>original: "ग्रहो मौढ्यं प्राप्तो मरणमचिरात् स्त्रीसुतधनैः प्रहीणत्वं व्यर्थे कलहमपवादं परिभवम् । समर्क्षस्यः खेटो न कलयति वैशेषिकफलं सुखं वा दुःखं वा जनयति यथापूर्वमचलम् ॥ १९ ॥"</note>

*Verse 19.* If a planet be <term>eclipsed</term> <note>Combust or hidden by the Sun's rays (Maudhya).</note> by the Sun’s rays the person born will, (during the said planet’s dasa period) meet with his end within a short time. His wife, children as well as his wealth will also be destroyed. He will <note>The text breaks off here, continuing the description of the eclipsed planet's effects on the next page.</note>

<summary>This page details the astrological consequences of planets situated in unfavorable conditions, such as inimical signs, signs of depression, or combustion by the Sun. These positions are predicted to bring about poverty, loss of status, social disgrace, and physical hardship during their respective time-periods.</summary>
<keywords>astrology, planetary positions, depression, combustion, dasa periods, inimical signs, social status, Vedic astrology</keywords>

<note>original: "वक्रं गतः स्वोच्चफलं विदध्यात्सपत्नीचर्क्षगतोऽपि खेटः । वर्गोत्तमांशस्थितखेचरोऽपि स्वक्षेत्रगस्योक्तफलानि तद्वत् ॥ २० ॥"</note>
*Verse 20* When a planet is retrograde in its motion <note>appearing to move backward from Earth's perspective</note>, the effects described as resulting from an occupation of its exaltation sign <note>the sign where a planet is strongest</note> should be predicted, even though the planet is posited in an inimical or depression sign <note>signs where the planet is traditionally weak</note>. The effects accruing for a planet situated in its own sign <gloss>its own house</gloss> should be ascribed to it similarly when it happens to occupy a <term>Vargottama amsa</term> <note>a position where a planet stays in the same sign in both the main chart and the 9th divisional chart, greatly increasing its strength</note>.

## ## Chapter 10

<note>original: "शुभाधिपयुतेक्षिते सुतकलत्रभे लग्नतो विधोरपि तयोः शुभं त्वितरथा न सिद्धिस्तयोः । सिताद्ययसुखाष्टगैः खरखगैःसन्मध्यगे सितेऽप्यथ शुभेतरेक्षितयुते च जायावधः ॥ १ ॥"</note>

->Chapter 10<-

*Verse 1* If the 5th house <note>representing children</note> and the 7th house <note>representing marriage</note> reckoned either from the Ascendant or the Moon be occupied or aspected by the lord of the 9th house <note>the house of fortune and grace</note>, then there is good for those two houses; otherwise, there is no success for them. If the two said houses are surrounded by malefic planets, or if Venus is placed between malefics, or is associated with or aspected by non-benefic planets, there will be the loss of the wife.

# Phaladeepika Adhyaya X

->When the seventh house or its lord is joined with, aspected by, or placed between malefic planets,<-
->or is in a sign of depression or an enemy's house, or is eclipsed by the Sun's rays, one should predict the loss of a wife.<-
->If Venus is with a malefic in the seventh, fifth, or ninth house, the man will be without a wife.<-
<margin>Verse 4, line 2</margin>
->If Venus is in a <term>Varga</term> <note>a divisional chart or subdivision of a sign</note> of Mars or Saturn, or is aspected by them, the man will be inclined toward the wives of others. || 4 ||<-

*Verse 4.* If the 7th house or its lord be associated with, aspected by or posited betwixt malefics <note>original: "malefics" refers to planets like Saturn or Mars that traditionally bring challenges</note> or be in depression or inimical sign, or eclipsed by the Sun's rays, loss of wife is certain. Venus in conjunction with a malefic occupying the 7th, 5th or 9th house will make the native bereft of a wife. When Venus is in a <term>Varga</term> <note>original: "वर्गे" - a specific division of the zodiac used to determine planetary strength</note> of Mars or Saturn or has the aspect of these planets, the person born will have a liaison with other people's wives.

---

->If Mars and Saturn are in the seventh house from Venus and the Moon, the man will be without a wife or childless.<-
->If a eunuch planet <note>referring to Mercury or Saturn in traditional astrology</note> is in the seventh and two planets are in the eleventh, there will be two wives.<-
->If the lord of the seventh and Venus are in dual signs or divisions, he will have two wives.<-
->One should determine the number of wives by the number of planets joined with these two. || 5 ||<-

*Verse 5.* If Venus and the Moon are in opposition to Mars and Saturn in any nativity, the person concerned will be either wifeless or issueless; when there is a hermaphrodite planet <note>a planet of neutral gender, typically Mercury or Saturn</note> in the 7th house and the 11th house is occupied by two planets, the person will have two wives. If the lord of the 7th house and Venus be each posited in a dual <term>Rasi</term> <note>Zodiac sign</note> or <term>Amsa</term> <note>a divisional portion of a sign</note>, the person will have two wives. Generally one ought to predict the number of wives in such cases by the number of the planets in conjunction with those two, *namely*, the lord of the 7th and Venus.

---

->The number of wives is determined by the planets in the seventh house; their longevity by the nature of those planets.<-
<margin>1</margin>
->If a strong benefic planet is in the seventh, the wife will be virtuous and blessed with good sons.<-

->Even a malefic planet, if situated in its own house in the 7th, brings good fortune; and if the lord of the 7th is in a state of desire <note>original: "kamasthito," referring to the 7th house or a state of passion</note> for the wife, it is auspicious.<-

*Verse 6.* It is through the number of planets in the 7th house <note>The 7th house in a birth chart traditionally governs marriage and serious partnerships.</note> that one ought to divine the number of women that a person may associate with. Of these, the number that will die at an early age will correspond to the number of planets in the 7th house that are malefic <note>term: malefic (an inauspicious planet like Mars or Saturn)</note>, while the number of benefics <note>term: benefic (an auspicious planet like Jupiter or Venus)</note> will denote the number that will survive. If the lord of the 7th be benefic and possessed of strength, the native will have a good-natured woman as his partner and be endowed with good children. Even a malefic will do good to the wife if he should be in the 7th owning that house. Benefics in the 7th will be productive of good unless they happen to be the lords of the 6th, 8th, and 12th houses <note>These three houses are known as "Dusthanas" or houses of suffering and loss.</note>.

->The destruction of the wife occurs when the 2nd and 7th houses are occupied or aspected by malefics;<-
->there, they say the aspect of a cruel planet is especially productive of evil results.<-
->In the same way, for the wife, there is a defect if such planets are in the 7th or 8th houses;<-
->but if these are aspected by or joined with benefics, the couple is fortunate.<-

*Verse 7.* When the 2nd and 7th houses are either occupied or aspected <note>term: aspected (when a planet "looks" at another house from its position)</note> by malefics there will be loss of wife; and the effect will invariably be untoward in the case of malefic aspects. Similar predictions about husbands may be made from the wife's nativity <note>term: nativity (birth chart)</note> when the 7th and 8th houses are likewise afflicted. But if the concerned two houses be aspected or occupied by benefics, the couple will be lucky and enjoy all comforts.

->When the Moon is with Saturn in the 7th house, one becomes a "Punarbhu" <note>original: "punarbhu," a woman who marries a second time</note>, or the husband may be childless or lose his wife.<-
->With malefics in the 7th house in debilitation or enemy signs, death of the wife or husband occurs, or when they are in the 8th or 2nd houses.<-

*Verse 8.* When the Moon along with Saturn occupies...

# Phaladeepika, Chapter 10

<page-num>108</page-num>
<header>Light on Fruitful Astrology, Chapter 10</header>

occupies the <term>7th house</term> <note>The house of marriage and partnerships</note>, the woman concerned will be remarried or the husband will be either wifeless or childless. If <term>malefics</term> <note>Naturally cruel planets like Mars or Saturn</note> in their <term>depression</term> <note>The sign where a planet is at its weakest; also called debilitation</note> or inimical house be posited in the 7th, the 8th, or the 2nd, the demise of both the woman and her husband should be predicted.

<margin>{</margin> *Verse 9.* When the <term>7th house</term> is an even sign <note>Signs like Taurus, Cancer, Virgo, etc., considered feminine</note> and if the lord of that house be also similarly situated (that is, in an even sign) and if <term>Venus</term> be not be overpowered by the <term>Sun's rays</term> <note>Combustion; when a planet is too close to the Sun to function effectively</note> and if the lords of the <term>5th</term> <note>House of children</note> and <term>7th houses</term> possess strength, the person concerned will certainly be blessed with wife and children.

->The birth sign of the wife will be in a trine from the sign or Navamsa occupied by the lords of the 1st and 7th, or their signs of exaltation or depression, or the sign with the most points in the Moon’s eight-fold chart.<-

*Verse 11.* Find the <term>Rasi</term> <note>Zodiac sign</note> and <term>Navamsa</term> <note>The ninth-part harmonic division of a sign</note> occupied by the lords of the <term>1st</term> <note>The Ascendant or self</note> and the <term>7th houses</term> in a person's <term>nativity</term> <note>Birth chart</note>. The <term>Janma Rasi</term> <note>Moon sign at birth</note> of the wife will be a sign triangular to the aforesaid Rasi and <term>Navamsa</term>, or it may be the <term>exaltation</term> <note>The sign where a planet is at its highest strength</note> or <term>depression</term> Rasi of the lords of the 1st and the 7th, or it will be that <term>Rasi</term> which contains the largest number of benefic dots in the <term>Moon's Ashtaka-varga</term> <note>A system of points used to calculate the strength of houses and planets</note> table of the husband.

# Chapter 10: Verses 12-15

->**The Direction of the Spouse and the Timing of Marriage**<-

The direction of the country from which the wife comes is indicated by the <term>Rasi</term><note>A sign of the zodiac</note> owned by whichever of these three planets is the strongest: (1) the planet occupying the 7th house, (2) the planet owning the 7th house, or (3) Venus <note>original: "Bhargava," a name for Venus as the descendant of the sage Bhrigu</note>. The marriage may be expected to occur when Venus or the lord of the 7th house, during its transit, passes through a sign that is in <term>Trine</term><note>A triangular relationship of 120 degrees (the 1st, 5th, or 9th positions)</note> to the Rasi or <term>Navamsa</term><note>The ninth-part harmonic chart used for fine-tuning predictions</note> occupied by the lord of the <term>Lagna</term><note>The Ascendant or 1st house</note>.
**— Verse 12**

The acquisition of a wife may happen during the <term>Dasa</term><note>A planetary major period or "time-lord" cycle</note> of the planet that (1) is positioned in the 7th house, (2) aspects <note>gazes upon or influences</note> the 7th house, or (3) owns the 7th house. This event may also occur when the lord of the Lagna, in its transit, reaches the sign representing the 7th house.
**— Verse 13**

Determine which planet is stronger in each of the following two pairs: (1) the lords of the Rasi and Navamsa occupied by the lord of the 7th house; or (2) Venus and the Moon. During the Dasa period of that stronger planet, when Jupiter <note>original: "Devaguru," the teacher of the gods</note> passes through a sign triangular to the Rasi or Navamsa occupied by the lord of the 7th house, the marriage ceremony <note>original: "Karagraha," literally 'taking the hand'</note> may be declared to take place.
**— Verse 14**

When the lord of the 7th house is positioned in an enemy's sign or a sign of its <term>Debilitation</term><note>The sign where a planet is at its weakest</note>, or if it is combust <note>hidden by the sun's rays</note> or aspected by malefic planets; and if the 7th house itself is occupied or aspected by malefics, the wise declare a loss of the spouse.
**— Verse 15**

->**Thus ends the Tenth Chapter titled "The Seventh House" in the Phaladeepika composed by Mantreswara.**<-

<summary>This concluding section of Chapter 10 provides specific astrological formulas for determining the direction of a spouse's home and the timing of marriage based on planetary transits and cycles. It also warns of planetary conditions that may lead to the loss of a partner.</summary>

<keywords>marriage timing, 7th house, Venus, Jupiter transits, Dasa periods, Phaladeepika, Mantreswara, spouse's direction, Navamsa, Lagna</keywords>

<header>110 Phaladeepika Chapter XI</header>

*Verse 15.* If the lord of the 7th house <note>The 7th house traditionally governs marriage and partnerships.</note> occupies an inimical or depression sign, or be eclipsed <note>Combust, or too close to the Sun.</note> or be aspected by malefics, and the 7th house itself be associated with or aspected by malefics, there will be loss of wife; so say the wise.

Thus ends the 10th Chapter on "the House of the Spouse" in the work *Phaladeepika* composed by Mantreswara.

# ॥ Chapter Eleven ॥

->Whatever influences in a male birth-chart are applicable to women, O Dear One, should be applied to them;<-
->Her widowhood or auspicious married state from the 8th, and children from the 9th, and her physical form from the Ascendant.<-
->Her husband and her attractiveness from the 7th house, and her conduct and chastity from the 4th;<-
->Benefics in these houses grant good results, while cruel planets grant evil, unless they are the lords of those houses. ॥ 1 ॥<-

## Chapter XI.

*Verse 1.* Whatever effect may accrue from the horoscope of females that is applicable only to men, should be ascribed to the husband. Her prosperity and happiness <note>Specifically *Mangalya*, referring to the longevity of the husband and her status as a married woman.</note> has to be deduced from the 8th place (from the Ascendant <term>Lagna: the rising sign at the time of birth</term> or the Moon, whichever is stronger). Children should be declared through the 9th house, and matters relating to her appearance, beauty, etc., should be determined from the Ascendant. It is from the 7th place that her welfare and the nature of the husband should be ascertained, while her associations and chastity should be predicted from an examination of the 4th house. Benefics <note>Favorable planets like Jupiter or Venus.</note> in these houses produce good results, while malefics <note>Harsh planets like Mars or Saturn.</note> in the above houses are productive of evil, unless they happen to own the houses, in which case the effect will be good.

<summary>This page concludes the tenth chapter on marriage and begins the eleventh chapter, which outlines the specific astrological houses used to interpret a woman's life, including her husband's longevity, her children, and her character.</summary>
<keywords>Phaladeepika, Mantreswara, Chapter XI, Verse, Ascendant, 7th house, 8th house, 9th house, 4th house, female horoscopy, marriage, chastity</keywords>

# Chapter Eleven

->If the Ascendant and the Moon are both in even signs and are viewed by benefics,<-
->she will have good sons, a fine husband, ornaments, wealth, and excellent character.<-
->If they are in odd signs and viewed by or joined with malefics, she will be masculine,<-
->deceitful, uncontrollable by her husband, fierce, and impoverished.<-

**Verse 2.** If both the <term>Ascendant</term> <note>The zodiac sign rising on the eastern horizon at the moment of birth</note> and the Moon are in even signs <note>Taurus, Cancer, Virgo, Scorpio, Capricorn, and Pisces</note> and be aspected by <term>benefic</term> <note>Naturally auspicious planets like Jupiter, Venus, or Mercury</note> planets, the woman born will bear good sons, possess an excellent husband and be well ornamented. She will be very prosperous and possess excellent qualities. If the <term>Lagna</term> <note>The Ascendant</note> and the Moon are in odd signs <note>Aries, Gemini, Leo, Libra, Sagittarius, and Aquarius</note> and be aspected by or associated with <term>malefic</term> <note>Naturally harsh planets like Mars or Saturn</note> planets, she will be masculine in her bearing, insincere, ungovernable and cruel beyond measure to her husband, and she will be poor.

->If the seventh house or its sub-division belongs to a benefic, the husband is illustrious,<-
->famous, learned, and wealthy; if the opposite, he is deformed, dull, a rogue, and poor.<-
->With fiery planets in the seventh, she becomes a widow; with mixed planets, she remarries.<-
->Malefics in the eighth kill the husband; benefics in the second, she dies first.<-

**Verse 3.** If the 7th house <note>The house governing marriage and partnerships</note> or setting <term>Navamsa</term> <note>A one-ninth sub-division of a zodiac sign used for fine-tuning predictions</note> be a sign owned by a benefic, the husband of the woman will be bright in appearance, famous, learned and wealthy. If it be otherwise <note>owned by a malefic</note>, he will be deformed, and stupid or be a gambler and will have lost all his wealth, and the couple will not live together. When malefic planets occupy the 7th <term>bhava</term> <note>House</note>, the female born will become a widow; if the planets in the 7th house be of a mixed sort <note>both auspicious and inauspicious</note>, she will remarry. If the 8th house <note>The house of longevity for the husband in a woman's chart</note> be occupied by malefics, she will cause the destruction of her husband. If benefics should occupy the 2nd house, she will herself die <note>before her husband</note>.

->If the Moon is in the fifth house in the signs of Scorpio, Virgo, or Leo, she has few children;<-
->If the seventh house is the sign of Saturn or Mars, she has a diseased womb.<-

# Phala Deepika, Chapter XI

->**If the fourth house is joined by malefic planets, or if Saturn and Mars occupy the fourth house, the woman becomes unchaste.**<-
->**If the Lagna and the Moon are in the signs of Saturn or Mars, and Venus is also there, she will be a profligate.**<-

*Verse 4.* When the Moon is in Scorpio, Virgo, Taurus, or Leo—provided these signs coincide with the 5th <term>Bhava</term> <note>The house of children and intelligence</note>—the woman concerned will have few children. If the 7th house <note>The house of marriage</note> or the setting <term>Navamsa</term> <note>A one-ninth divisional chart used for fine-tuning predictions</note> belongs to Saturn, Mars, or the Sun, the woman will have a diseased womb. If <term>malefic</term> <note>Harmful or challenging planets like Mars and Saturn</note> planets be in the 4th house, the female born will become unchaste. If Saturn and Mars occupy each other's <term>Rasi</term> <note>Zodiac sign</note> or <term>Amsa</term> <note>Divisional portion</note> and if the <term>Lagna</term> <note>The Ascendant or rising sign</note>, the Moon, and Venus be also associated there, she will, on being prompted, go to other men.

->**If the seventh house or its Navamsa belongs to a benefic, she will have beautiful hips and be fortunate.**<-
->**If the Moon is in a good junction and the Lagna and fourth house are strong, she is highly virtuous.**<-
->**If benefics are in the trines, she is happy, has children, wealth, and virtue.**<-
->**If malefics are there or benefics are weak, she is barren or her children die.**<-

*Verse 5.* If the 7th house or the setting Navamsa belongs to a <term>benefic</term> <note>Auspicous planets like Jupiter or Venus</note> planet, the woman born will be beautiful, possess handsome hips, and be very fortunate. If the Moon, the Lagna, and the 4th house be associated with benefics, the woman concerned will be of a virtuous disposition and be endowed with very good qualities. If benefics are posited in <term>Trikona</term> <note>Trine houses, specifically the 1st, 5th, and 9th</note> houses, she will be happy, possess children, wealth, and be good-natured. If the benefics in question be weak, or if the said houses be occupied by malefics, the woman will become barren or her offspring will all die early.

->**When the Moon is in a sign of Mars, and in the Trimsamsas of Mars and others respectively,**<-
->**She is wicked, a servant, well-behaved, a deceiver, or a slanderer.**<-
->**In a sign of Venus: many vices, following other men, respected, wise, or steady.**<-
->**In a sign of Mercury: a pretender, like a hermaphrodite, sweet-spoken, virtuous, or restless.**<-

*Verse 6.* <note>This verse describes the effects of the Moon's position in specific 30th-part divisions called Trimsamsas. The original Sanskrit text lists the outcomes based on which planet rules that specific degree.</note>

# Chapter Eleven
## Verses 6-7
### Page 113

*Verse 6.* When the Moon is in a <term>Rasi<note>A zodiac sign, representing a 30-degree arc of the sky.</note></term> belonging to Mars <note>Aries or Scorpio</note>, the woman born will be (1) ill-behaved (2) a menial or slave and unchaste (3) virtuous and lofty-minded (4) deceitful and (5) wicked according as the <term>Trimsamsa<note>A specific one-degree or "thirtieth" division of a sign used in Vedic astrology to determine character traits.</note></term> of the Moon belongs to (1) Mars (2) Saturn (3) Jupiter (4) Mercury and (5) Venus respectively. 

When the Moon is in <term>Vrishabha<note>Taurus</note></term> or <term>Tula<note>Libra</note></term>, the corresponding effects are: (1) she will be very wicked (2) she will resort to a second husband (3) she will be highly respected (4) very intelligent and (5) famous. 

When the Moon occupies a house of Mercury <note>Gemini or Virgo</note>, the respective effects are: (1) she will be dishonest (2) she will be a <term>eunuch<note>In this astrological context, likely referring to a woman who is sterile or lacks typical feminine traits.</note></term> (3) virtuous (4) endowed with all good qualities and (5) repining.

->Self-willed, a husband-slayer, endowed with great qualities, skilled in crafts, or virtuous;<-
->In the Moon's or Jupiter's signs: rich in virtues, detached, highly talented, skilled in arts, or very holy;<-
->In Saturn's signs: a servant, attached to others, devoted to her husband, unchaste, or childless;<-
->Deceitful, of low conduct, a queen, or behaving like a man and attached to others.<-

*Verse 7.* If the Moon is posited in <term>Kataka<note>Cancer</note></term>, the effects in their order are: (1) she will be self-willed and uncontrolled (2) she will kill her husband (3) she will be endowed with many good qualities (4) she will be skilled in the arts and (5) she will be virtuous. 

If the Moon occupies a sign of Jupiter <note>Sagittarius or Pisces</note>, the effect will be to make the woman born (1) to be endowed with many good qualities (2) not to indulge much in coition (3) to possess many good qualities (4) skilled in the arts and (5) very chaste. 

When the Moon is in a sign owned by Saturn <note>Capricorn or Aquarius</note>, the several effects will respectively be to make the female born (1) a maid servant (2) to be attached to another man (3) devoted to her husband (4) wicked and (5) barren.

If the Moon is in **Leo** <note>original: "Simha"</note>, the effects of the Moon being posited in the several **degrees of a sign** <note>term: Trimsamsa (a 1/30th division of a zodiac sign used to determine specific character traits)</note> will respectively be to make the woman born (1) a wicked wife (2) a person of low behavior (3) the wife of a king (4) of a masculine disposition and (5) to be attached to a man other than her husband.

->The results arising from the degrees occupied by the Moon or the Ascendant,<-
->should be weighed according to the relative strength of the two.<-

*Verse 8* <note>original: "Sloka"</note> The effects described as due to the **degree** <note>Trimsamsa</note> occupied by the Moon at birth or those mentioned for the **degree** rising at the time <note>the Ascendant</note> will come to pass according as the one or the other is stronger.

->Those who know the science say that women born under Jyeshta, Aslesha, Moola, and Visakha<-
->destroy respectively the elder brother, the mother, the father, and the husband's younger brother.<-
->Those born under Chitra, Ardra, Aslesha, Jyeshta, Satabhishak, Moola, Krithika, and Pushya<-
->are barren, widowed, lose their children, are abandoned by their beloved, or lack wealth.<-

*Verse 9* Astrologers <note>original: "tajjna" - those who know</note> declare that (1) the eldest brother (2) the mother (3) the father and (4) the youngest brother respectively of a person will die (soon after the marriage) if his wife be born under the stars <note>term: Nakshatras (lunar mansions)</note> (1) **Jyeshta** (2) **Aslesha** (3) **Moola** and (4) **Visakha**. Females born under the asterisms **Chitra, Ardra, Aslesha, Jyeshta, Satabhishak, Moola, Krithika** and **Pushya** will be barren, become widows, be mothers of children that are dead, will have been cast away by their husbands or be without wealth.

->If the lords of the Moon, the 7th house, and the Ascendant are with benefics, well-placed and bright,<-
->the woman is honored by kin, skilled in virtuous deeds, beautiful, and fortunate.<-
->She is dear to her husband, has good sons, is of noble character, and remains<-
->a virtuous wife with a long-lived husband and a beautiful body, provided the 8th house is blessed.<-

# Verse 10-11 Chapter Eleven 115

*Verse 10.* If the lords of (1) the <term>Lagna</term> <note>the Ascendant or the sign rising on the eastern horizon at birth</note>, (2) the 9th house and (3) the sign occupied by the Moon be associated with benefics and are posited in good houses and be of brilliant rays (<gloss>i.e.</gloss> not be eclipsed by the sun's proximity), the woman concerned will be held in high esteem by her relations. She will do many good deeds, will be very handsome and be prosperous. She will please her husband, bear good sons and be virtuous in her disposition, the period of this happy life with her husband depending on the strength derived by the 8th house from benefics (either by association or aspect).

->When the Moon is in a non-upachaya sign and is aspected by Mars,<-
->the menses of a woman become fruitful for pregnancy; otherwise, they are fruitless.<-
->When the Moon is in her own upachaya sign and aspected by Jupiter, the man should perform the act of impregnation<-
->at an auspicious time, enriched by benefics and free from forbidden periods like Parva. || 11 ||<-

->|| Thus ends the eleventh chapter named "Female Horoscopy" in the Phaladeepika composed by Mantreswara. ||<-
<note>original: "iti mantreśvaraviracitāyāṃ phaladīpikāyāṃ strījātakanāmaikādaśo'dhyāyaḥ"</note>

*Verse 11.* If a woman's menses set in when the Moon is in an <term>Anupachaya</term> <note>Non-improving houses: the 1st, 2nd, 4th, 5th, 7th, 8th, 9th, and 12th</note> place and aspected by Mars, they become favourable to conception and not otherwise. The husband should sow the seed when the Moon occupies an <term>Upachaya</term> <note>Improving houses: the 3rd, 6th, 10th, and 11th</note> house (in respect to the female's nativity) and is aspected by Jupiter. This is to be done at an unexceptionable <term>lagna</term> <note>auspicious moment/ascendant</note> with many good points in its favour and unconnected with <term>Parva</term> <note>Lunar festival days or transitions like the full/new moon, which are considered spiritually sensitive</note> and other objectionable periods of time.

Thus ends the 11th Chapter on “the Horoscopes of women” in the work *Phaladeepika* composed by *Mantreswara*.

## ## Chapter XII

->If Jupiter and the lords of the 5th house from the Ascendant <note>original: "Lagna"</note> and the Moon are well-placed; if the 5th house is joined or aspected by the lord of an auspicious house or a benefic; and if the lords of the Ascendant and the 5th house are together, aspect each other, or exchange houses, the attainment of children is certain.<-

### Chapter XII.

*Verse 1* If Jupiter and the lords of the 5th house reckoned from the Ascendant and the Moon be well placed, and when the 5th house has on it the aspect of a benefic planet or of one owning an auspicious house (i.e., other than the 6th, 8th and 12th), or if the lords of the Ascendant and the 5th house be posited together in a house or have mutual benefic aspect or occupy each other's places, the acquisition of children is assured. <note>The 5th house is the primary house governing children in Vedic astrology.</note>

->When the 5th houses from the Ascendant, Jupiter, and the Moon are joined or aspected by malefics and lack the influence of benefics; or if these houses are hemmed in by malefics and their lords are in bad houses, children will not be born by any means.<-

*Verse 2.* When the 5th places counted from the Ascendant, Jupiter and the Moon are either associated with or aspected by malefics <note>Malefics typically include Mars, Saturn, Rahu, and Ketu.</note> and are devoid of benefics or of their aspect, or, when these houses are surrounded by malefics on both sides and when the lords of the above said houses are posited in Bad Houses <term>Dusstthanas: the 6th, 8th, and 12th houses which represent obstacles, illness, and loss</term>, the person concerned can have no issues whatever.

->If a malefic is in its own sign in the 5th, one has children; if joined by another malefic, children are many. If a benefic is in its own sign in the 5th, it may destroy children. Scorpio, Taurus, Virgo, and Leo are known as "signs of few children"; if the 5th house falls in these, one has few children and attains them late in life.<-

*Verse 3.* If a malefic planet owning the 5th house be in that house, the person concerned will have children. When that house is occupied by a malefic, the person will have sons in plenty. If a benefic planet should occupy its own sign in the 5th, it acts as a destroyer of children <note>This is a specific astrological paradox where a benefic in its own 5th house can sometimes be unfavorable for progeny.</note>. The signs Scorpio, Taurus, Virgo, and Leo are known as "signs of few children." If one of these signs becomes the 5th house, the person will have few children and will obtain them only after a long time.

<summary>This page introduces the astrological conditions for the birth of children, detailing planetary combinations that either guarantee or deny progeny. It specifically identifies the 5th house, Jupiter, and the Ascendant as the primary factors for evaluation.</summary>
<keywords>progeny, 5th house, Jupiter, Ascendant, Phaladeepika, Vedic astrology, malefics, benefics, infertility, signs of few children</keywords>

->Chapter Twelve, Verses 4-5<-

occupy the 5th house and at the same time own that sign or be in exaltation there, the result is loss of children. The signs <term>Vrischika</term> <note>Scorpio</note>, <term>Vrishabha</term> <note>Taurus</note>, <term>Kanya</term> <note>Virgo</note> and <term>Simha</term> <note>Leo</note> when they happen to represent the 5th house are termed childless <term>Rasis</term> <note>Zodiac signs</note> and a person born under such a condition will be of very limited <term>progeny</term> <note>offspring</note> and this too will be accomplished only after a long interval.

> <note>original Sanskrit: "सूर्ये चाल्पसुतर्क्षगे..."</note>
> When the Sun is in a sign that yields few children, Saturn is in the 8th house, and Mars is in the Ascendant;
> Or when Saturn, Jupiter, and Mars are in the Ascendant, 8th, and 12th houses respectively, and the 5th house is a childless sign;
> Or when the Moon is in the 11th house, and the 5th house from Jupiter contains a malefic planet, while many planets occupy the Ascendant;
> In these cases, one obtains a child only after a long time and through great effort.

*Sloka 4.* <note>Verse 4</note> When the Sun is posited in the 5th house identical with a childless <term>Rasi</term>, <term>Saturn</term> is in the 8th and <term>Mars</term> in the <term>Lagna</term> <note>the Ascendant or the sign rising on the eastern horizon at birth</note>; or when Saturn, <term>Jupiter</term> and Mars occupy respectively the Lagna, the 8th and the 12th, and the 5th house happens to be a childless sign; or when the <term>Moon</term> occupies the 11th, and the 5th house reckoned from Jupiter is occupied by a <term>malefic planet</term> <note>a planet considered naturally unfavorable, such as Mars or Saturn</note> and there are many planets stationed in the Lagna; the person concerned will have a child late in life after a great effort.

> <note>original Sanskrit: "सूर्ये नान्ययुते सुतर्क्षसहिते..."</note>
> If the Sun alone occupies the 5th house in the sign of the Moon (Cancer),
> Or if Mars or Venus are there, children may be obtained through a second wife.
> If Saturn or the Moon are there, one has many children; if Mercury, few;
> If Jupiter is in the Moon's sign in the 5th, there are many daughters.

*Sloka 5.* <note>Verse 5</note> If the Sun should singly (without being associated with any other planet) occupy the 5th house identical with <term>Kataka</term> <note>the sign of Cancer</note>, there is possibility of the person concerned having children by a second wife. The same should be predicted if Mars or <term>Venus</term> be similarly placed.

Saturn in such a position will give many children. The Moon or Mercury similarly placed will not give many, while Jupiter in the Moon's sign <note>Cancer</note> identical with the 5th house will make the person concerned the father of many daughters.

->If malefics, Venus, and the Moon are in the 4th, 7th, and 10th houses;<-
->if malefics are in the 12th, 8th, 5th, and 1st houses;<-
->if Venus and Mercury are in the 7th, Jupiter in the 5th, and malefics in the 4th;<-
->or if the Moon is in the 5th and malefics are in the 8th, 12th, and 1st, the family becomes extinct.<-

*Verse 6* <note>original: "Sloha 6"</note> The following 4 <term>yogas</term> <note>astrological combinations</note> lead to family extinction: (1) The 4th, the 7th and the 10th houses in any nativity being occupied respectively by a malefic planet, Venus and the Moon, (2) the 12th, the 8th, the 5th and the 1st houses being occupied by malefics, (3) Venus and Mercury in the 7th, Jupiter in the 5th and malefics in the 4th houses, and (4) the Moon in the 5th and malefics in the 8th, the 12th and the 1st.

->If a malefic is in the Ascendant, the Ascendant lord is in the 5th, the 5th lord is in the 3rd, and the Moon is in the 4th, one is childless.<-
->If the Moon is in an odd sign in the 5th and aspected by the Sun, one suffers through children or is <unclear>childless</unclear>.<-

*Verse 7* <note>original: "Sloha 7"</note> The following two <term>yogas</term> lead to affliction through children or barrenness: (1) a malefic in the <term>Lagna</term> <note>the Ascendant or 1st house</note>, its lord in the 5th, the lord of the 5th in the 3rd, and the Moon in the 4th, (2) the Moon in an odd <term>Rasi</term> <note>Zodiac sign</note> or <term>Amsa</term> <note>divisional sign</note> in the 5th and aspected by the Sun.

->If the 5th house belongs to Saturn or Mercury and is joined or seen by Mandi or Saturn,<-
->and if the lord of the 5th is weak and unrelated to the Ascendant lord, there is an adopted son.<-

*Verse 8* <note>original: "Sloha 8"</note> If the 5th <term>bhava</term> <note>astrological house</note> be a sign owned by Saturn or Mercury and is aspected by or associated with Mandi <note>a sensitive mathematical point considered malefic</note> or Saturn, the person concerned will have a son by adoption. The same will be the result when the lord <insert>of the 5th is weak and has no connection with the lord of the Ascendant.</insert>

<summary>This page details specific planetary configurations (yogas) that indicate family extinction, childlessness, or the adoption of a son. It emphasizes the roles of Saturn, Mercury, and the 5th house in determining these outcomes.</summary>
